Let's use continuous optimization intuition: We can test families in code at runtime. We can include a phase that evaluates many analytic profiles: power law (i+ε)^p, exponential, Gaussian, beta, piecewise, and picks best. Then local search.

Since we can't run, code should be robust to choose. We can use random grid search over parameters for a set of parametric families using fast FFT, no need to know results. Then local search.

Parametric families (all nonnegative, length n, normalized):
- Power: (x+ε)^p with x=i/(n-1), p in [-0.5,2], ε small (e.g., 0.01, 0.1,1).
- Modified power with edge boost: ((x+ε1)*(1-x+ε2))^p? symmetric variants.
- Exponential: exp(λ x) with λ in [-5,5].
- Cosine bump: 1 + ε cos(π x + φ) nonnegative if ε≤1 etc.
- Beta-like: x^{a-1}(1-x)^{b-1} with a,b in [0.5,5]. Using endpoints may create large edge coefficients; but search.
- Product of powers: (x+ε)^p1 * (1-x+ε)^p2 (asymmetric).
- Polynomial RBF: exp(-((x-mu)/sigma)^2) + floor.
- Binomial prefix times exp(θ x + φ x² + ψ sin(2π r x)): tune.

We can use multiple types of moves:
1. Multiplicative subgradient (pressure) moves:
   - Compute c = convolution(a,a) via FFT (or exact for score?). Use np.convolve for exact maybe slower; use FFT surrogate for pressure and score approximate, then exact when promising.

For binomial, lower half =U, tail slightly lower; with threshold 0.9U, active includes many lower + some tail.
   - Compute pressure p_i = ∑_j a_j active[j+i] (valid j). Use FFT: Let A_pad length N, Active_pad length N. p_i = ∑_j a_j active_{i+j}. We can compute via convolution of a with reversed active? Let's derive an easy method using np.convolve for length n and active length 2n-1? Direct: For each i, p_i = dot(a, active[i: i+n]) (since j=0..n-1, k=i+j ranges i..i+n-1). This is a sliding window dot product. We can compute with convolution: p = np.convolve(a, active, mode='valid')? np.convolve(a, active, 'valid') returns length len(active)-n+1 = n. For zero-based, conv(a, active, valid)[i] = ∑_{m=0}^{n-1} a[m] active[i + (n-1-m)]? Depending on definition. np.convolve: c[k]=∑_i a[i] v[k-i]. For 'valid' with len v >= len a, output k=0..len(v)-len(a): c[k]=∑_{i=0}^{n-1} a[i] v[k-i]. Need v index nonnegative: if k from0, v[-i] invalid? Actually np.convolve uses zero padding; c[k]=∑_{i=0}^{n-1} a[i] v[k-i]. For k=0, includes v[0], v[-1]... invalid zero. That gives high lags, not what we want. Use np.correlate(active, a, mode='valid')? np.correlate(v,a) = ∑ a_i v[i+k]? Documentation: correlate(a,v)[k]=∑ a_i conj(v_{i+k})? If use np.correlate(active, a, mode='valid'), output k=0..n-1: ∑_{i=0}^{n-1} active[i+k] a[i] => exactly p_k. Good. So p = np.correlate(active, a, mode='valid') (a length n, active length 2n-1). Need active shape (2n-1,). This is O(n*(2n)? np.correlate direct maybe O(n²) in C, but n=6733 =>45M, okay per iteration? If many iterations, maybe heavy.    - Update y_i = log(a_i+eps) - η * p_i / max(p). Then a = softmax(y). To avoid underflow, use y - max(y). Step η from 0.01 to 0.5. If score improves (exact or surrogate), update.

Need implement fast score surrogate:
```
def _fftconvolve_squared(a):
    n = len(a)
    N = 1
    while N < 2*n-1: N <<=1
    fa = np.fft.rfft(a, n=N)
    c = np.fft.irfft(fa*fa, n=N)[:2*n-1]
    return c
```
Score = 2*n*max(c)/sum(a)^2. Since we normalize sum=1 often, score=2n max(c). Need ensure irfft real no negative tiny due to rounding; clip c to 0 if small negative. For exact, use evaluate_sequence.

Parametric family evaluator: Build x=np.linspace(0,1,n) once. For many candidates, compute vector. Use np.clip min 1e-300. Normalize.

```

Block moves:
```
def _block_move(a, rng, sigma):
    cand=a.copy()
    width = rng.integers(1, max(2, n//20)) or log-uniform in [1,n//5]
    start=rng.integers(0,n-width)
    cand[start:start+width] *= np.exp(rng.normal(0, sigma))
    cand=np.clip(cand,0,1000)
    S=cand.sum()
    if S>0: cand/=S
    return cand
```
Use sigma values [0.1,0.3,1,3] depending time. Also "block shift": copy a block from another region? Maybe not.

Coordinate/subgradient move can be applied to a parameterization y (log a) and we should keep y of best. After acceptance, update y = log(cand+eps). For block moves, update y too.

Let's design final code structure:

Top-level helpers:
- `_fft_conv_square(a)`: returns convolution length 2n-1 via rfft. Use N power of two >=2n-1.
- `_fft_conv_square_score(a, n)`: returns (c, score) maybe. score uses sum.
- `_pressure_from_active(a, active)`: uses FFT to compute p_i = ∑ a_j active[i+j]. Returns p length n.
- `_softmax(y)`: safe.
- `_normalize(a)`: clip nonneg, sum 1.
- `_pressure_update(a, y, eta, active_gap, top_k, rng)`: compute c, active, p, update y, return cand,y.
- `_block_update(a,rng,sigma)`.
- `_transfer_update(a, p_pressure, rng)` maybe uses pressure.

Subgradient iteration steps:
```
def _pressure_update(a, y, eta, top_k, rng):
    c, S = _fft_conv_square(a)
    U = c.max()
    if U <=0: return a,y
    # active = top top_k or threshold?
    top = min(len(c), max(1, top_k))
    # To save partition, use threshold = np.partition(c, len(c)-top)[-1]
    thr = np.partition(c, len(c)-top)[-1]
    active = (c >= thr * (1 - 0.0?))  # ensure at least top? If duplicates, may > top.
    # maybe limit count to top by random? Not necessary.
    p = _pressure_fft(a, active)
    pmax = p.max()
    if pmax <=0: return a,y
    y_new = y - eta * (p / pmax)
    # add small noise to break symmetry
    y_new += rng.normal(0, 1e-5, a.shape)
    cand = _softmax(y_new)
    return cand, y_new
```
`np.partition` each iter length 13k cheap. active count may exceed top if many ties; fine.

Now, code details for safe numerical:
- `_normalize(a)`: a = np.asarray(a,float); a[~np.isfinite(a)] =0; a = np.clip(a,0,1000); S=a.sum(); if S>0 and finite: a = a/S (sum=1); else set uniform. Return.
- `_softmax(y)`: y = np.where(np.isfinite(y), y, -700); y -= y.max(); exp; if sum 0 return uniform; return exp/sum.
- `_fft_conv_square(a, N=None)`: if N None compute. Use `fa=np.fft.rfft(a, N)`. `c=np.fft.irfft(fa*fa, N)[:2*n-1]`. Due to roundoff, c may have small negative; `c = np.maximum(c, 0.0)`.
- `_surrogate_score(a)`: normalize? Input should normalized but do S=a.sum(); if S<1e-300 return inf; c=...; return 2*n*max(c)/S². To avoid normalization overhead, assume normalized and use 2*n*max; but block/softmax normalized. Use S for safety.
- `_pressure_fft(a, active)`: Ncalc power of two >=3*n-2. `ar = a[::-1]`. `fa = np.fft.rfft(ar, Ncalc)`; `fv = np.fft.rfft(active.astype(float), Ncalc)`; `conv = np.fft.irfft(fa*fv, Ncalc)`; `p = conv[n-1:2*n-1]`; `p = np.maximum(p,0)`. Is active length 2n-1; yes. If active boolean, astype float.
Check orientation again: ar conv active full. We need p_i = ∑ a_j active[i+j]. conv[ m ] with m = i+n-1. Using ar index r: ar[r]=a[n-1-r]. conv[m]=∑_{r=0}^{rmax} ar[r] active[m-r-m? Wait conv full definition np/FFT: (ar * active)[m] = ∑_{r} ar[r] active[m-r] (linear). Let r = n-1-j. Then ar[r]=a[j]. We need active[i+j]. Set m-r = i+j => m - (n-1-j)=i+j => m = i+n-1. Good. Sum over j valid becomes sum over r. So p_i=conv[i+n-1]. Our slice n-1:2n-1 gives i=0..n-1. Good.

But Ncalc power of two; circular convolution length N, if N>=len(ar)+len(active)-1 = n+(2n-1)-1=3n-2, no wrap. Use Ncalc>=3n-2.

Annealing/local search code:
```
def _local_search(a0, rng, deadline, current_global_score):
    a = _normalize(a0)
    best = a.copy(); best_score = _surrogate_score(best)
    best_exact = current_global_score? We'll evaluate best exact at start if finite.
    y = np.log(np.maximum(a,1e-300))
    t_anneal = 0.05
    no_improve = 0
    last_exact_time = time.monotonic()
    global_best_local = current_global_score
    best_global_local = a0? We'll return best if exact improved.
    top_k_options = [512,1024,2048,4096,7168, 2*n-1]
    eta_options = [0.005,0.01,0.02,0.05,0.1]
    sigma_options = [0.1,0.3,0.6,1.2,2.5]
    iter=0
    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
       iter +=1
       if iter % 1000 ==0: t_anneal *=0.9
       if iter % 2000 ==0: top_k = ... random
       r = rng.random()
       if r < 0.6:
           eta = rng.choice(eta_options)
           top_k = min(2*n-1, int(rng.choice(top_k_options)))
           cand, y_new = _pressure_update(a, y, eta, top_k, rng)
       elif r < 0.85:
           sigma = rng.choice(sigma_options)
           cand = _block_update(a, rng, sigma)
           y_new = np.log(np.maximum(cand,1e-300))
       else:
           cand = _transfer_update(a, rng)
           y_new = np.log(np.maximum(cand,1e-300))
       cand = _normalize(cand)
       score = _surrogate_score(cand)
       if not finite: continue
       delta = score - best_score
       if delta < 0 or (t_anneal>1e-6 and rng.random() < np.exp(-delta/t_anneal)):
           a=cand; y=y_new
           if delta < -1e-12:
               best=a.copy(); best_score=score; no_improve=0
           else:
               no_improve +=1
           # exact if improved best or periodically
           if (delta < -1e-10 and time.monotonic()-last_exact_time > 10) or (iter % 2000 ==0):
               exact = evaluate_sequence(best.tolist())
               if finite and exact < global_best_local:
                   global_best_local=exact; best_global_local=best.copy()
               last_exact_time=time.monotonic()
       else:
           no_improve +=1
           if no_improve > 3000:
               # strong restart: take best with big block noise and set as current (even if worse)
               a = _block_update(best, rng, 2.0)
               y = np.log(np.maximum(a,1e-300))
               no_improve=0
    # final exact on best
    exact = evaluate_sequence(best.tolist())
    if exact < global_best_local: ...
    return best_global_local, global_best_local
```
Need `_transfer_update`:
```
def _transfer_update(a, rng):
   cand = a.copy()
   # choose i,j; favor moving from high value to low? Could use rng with exponent weights.
   i = int(rng.integers(0,len(cand)-1)); j=...
   # to be useful, choose i from top 30% values, j from bottom 30%? Use random but if a[i] > a[j] maybe.
   # Use random but ensure before > after.
   while a[i] <= a[j] and attempts<10: choose...
   frac = rng.uniform(0.01,0.25)
   amt = frac * a[i]
   if amt <=0 or amt > a[i]: continue original
   cand[i]-=amt; cand[j]+=amt
   return _normalize(cand)
```
This may not be very effective but okay.

Block update width: Use log-uniform from 1 to n//4. `width = int(np.exp(rng.uniform(0, np.log(max(2,n//4)))))`. If width >=n width=n-1. start random. Multiply block by exp(normal(0,sigma)). Use sometimes add small noise to all: if rng.random<0.1: cand *= exp(normal(0,sigma/n?)). No.

We can also "mirror" block: after block multiply, with prob 0.2 symmetrize: cand = 0.5*(cand+cand[::-1])? But symmetry may not help. Keep asymmetric.

Param phase details:
```
def _param_search(n, x, rng, time_limit):
    candidates = [] # list of (score, arr)
    def consider(a):
       a=_normalize(a)
       if not finite: return
       s=_surrogate_score(a)
       if s finite: add to top 6
    # explicit candidates
    ...
    # random polynomial phase
    for base in [np.ones(n), p_binom, np.arange(1,n+1)]:
       for _ in range(1500):
          theta = rng.normal(0,1,6)
          a = base * np.exp(np.polynomial.polynomial.polyval(x, theta).clip(-20,20))
          consider(a)
    return top arrays
```
Using `np.polynomial.polynomial.polyval` may be slower; manually compute:
```
v = np.zeros(n)
for coef in theta[:6]: v = v + coef * x**power
```

```
def _optimize_poly(base, x, xp, n, starting, T, maxiter):
    def fun(theta):
        v = np.zeros(x.shape)
        for i in range(len(theta)):
            v = v + float(theta[i]) * xp[i]
        v = np.clip(v, -20, 20)
        a = base * np.exp(v)
        a = _normalize(a)
        return _surrogate_score(a) # exact max, not smooth; minimize Powell maybe
    # use Powell derivative-free, because fun non-smooth
    res = minimize(fun, starting, method='Powell', options={'maxiter':200, 'ftol':1e-8, 'xtol':1e-8, 'disp':False})
    return res.x
```

Let's write `_local_search` carefully:

```
def _local_search(a0, rng, deadline, start_exact_score):
    n = len(a0)
    a = _normalize(a0)
    best = a.copy()
    best_score = _surrogate_score(best)
    best_exact = start_exact_score if np.isfinite(start_exact_score) else np.inf
    best_exact_a = best.copy()
    y = np.log(np.maximum(a, 1e-300))
    anneal_t = 0.05
    no_improve = 0
    last_exact = time.monotonic()
    etas = np.array([0.002,0.005,0.01,0.02,0.05,0.1])
    gaps = np.array([0.02,0.05,0.1,0.2,0.3,0.5])
    sigmas = np.array([0.1,0.25,0.5,1.0,2.0])
    it=0
    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
       it +=1
       if it % 2000 ==0:
          anneal_t *=0.85
          if anneal_t <1e-5: anneal_t=1e-5
       move = rng.random()
       if move <0.62:
          eta = float(etas[int(rng.integers(len(etas)))])
          gap = float(gaps[int(rng.integers(len(gaps)))])
          cand, y_new = _pressure_update(a, y, eta, gap, rng)
       elif move <0.86:
          sigma = float(sigmas[int(rng.integers(len(sigmas)))])
          cand = _block_update(a, rng, sigma)
          y_new = np.log(np.maximum(cand,1e-300))
       else:
          cand = _transfer_update(a, rng)
          y_new = np.log(np.maximum(cand,1e-300))
       if cand is None: continue
       cand = _normalize(cand)
       score = _surrogate_score(cand)
       if not np.isfinite(score): continue
       delta = score - best_score
       accept = False
       if delta < -1e-12:
          accept = True
       else:
          if anneal_t > 0 and delta < 5*anneal_t:
             # compute prob safely
             prob = float(np.exp(-delta / anneal_t)) if delta/anneal_t < 700 else 0.0
             if rng.random() < prob: accept=True
       if accept:
          a = cand
          y = y_new
          if delta < -1e-12:
             best = a.copy()
             best_score = score
             no_improve=0
             # exact update if score significantly below current exact best or periodic
             now=time.monotonic()
             if (score < best_exact - 0.002) or (now - last_exact > 30.0):
                ex = evaluate_sequence(best.tolist())
                if np.isfinite(ex) and ex < best_exact:
                    best_exact = ex
                    best_exact_a = best.copy()
                last_exact = now
          else:
             no_improve +=1
       else:
          no_improve +=1
       if no_improve > 4000:
          # inject large perturbation to current a (not best) to escape
          a = _block_update(best, rng, 2.0)
          y = np.log(np.maximum(a,1e-300))
          no_improve = 0
    # final evaluations
    final_candidates = [best, best_exact_a]
    # maybe also current a
    best_final = best_exact_a
    best_final_score = best_exact
    for arr in final_candidates:
       ex = evaluate_sequence(arr.tolist())
       if np.isfinite(ex) and ex < best_final_score:
          best_final_score=ex; best_final=arr
    return best_final, best_final_score
```

This calls exact at final for best and exact_best (2 calls). If best_exact is inf maybe not.

But inside loop, `score < best_exact -0.002` uses surrogate vs exact; okay. If best_exact high, many exact calls. The `now-last_exact >30` ensures at least once 30s. Good. Could call 100 exact over 1800; okay.

Potential problem: If no local exact improvement but surrogate improves, best_exact remains start; at end eval best improves. Good.

Pressure update returns y_new; if y contains -inf for zeros, subtract pressure? We use max floor; fine.

`_pressure_update` should avoid excessive time if active count tiny. Code:
```
def _pressure_update(a, y, eta, gap, rng):
    n=len(a)
    c = _fft_conv_square(a)
    U = float(c.max())
    if not finite or U <=0: return a, y
    thr = U * (1.0 - gap)
    active = c >= thr
    if int(active.sum()) == 0:
        active[int(c.argmax())] = True
    # optional randomization if active count > 10000? no
    p = _pressure_fft(a, active)
    pmax = float(p.max())
    if pmax <= 1e-300:
        return a, y
    y2 = y - eta * (p / pmax)
    # y2 = np.clip(y2, -50, 50)
    y2 += float(rng.normal(0.0, 1e-6)) * np.random? Need vector noise: y2 += rng.normal(0, 1e-4, n)? Could be costly but fine. Use small noise to break ties.
    cand = _softmax(y2)
    return cand, y2
```
Use noise array `rng.normal(0.0, 1e-5, n)`.

Now, code for `_insert_top`:
```
def _insert_top(top, score, arr, limit):
    if not np.isfinite(score): return
    i=0
    while i < len(top):
        if score < top[i][0]:
            break
        i += 1
    top.insert(i, (score, arr))
    if len(top) > limit:
        top.pop()
```

def _normalize(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    if a.size == 0: return np.array([])
    a = a.copy()
    a[~np.isfinite(a)] = 0.0
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s > 0.0 and np.isfinite(s):
       a = a / s
    else:
       a = np.full(a.shape, 1.0/max(1,a.size))
    return a
```
If a size n, uniform sum1.

`_fft_conv_square`:
```
def _fft_conv_square(a):
    n=a.size
    need=2*n-1
    N=1
    while N < need: N <<=1
    fa=np.fft.rfft(a, N)
    c=np.fft.irfft(fa*fa, N)[:need]
    # remove tiny negatives
    c[np.abs(c)<1e-15]=0.0
    if c.min() <0: c = np.maximum(c,0.0)
    return c
```
For n=1, need=1, N=1, fine.

```

def _block_update(a, rng, sigma):
   n=a.size
   cand=a.copy()
   maxw=max(1,n//4)
   width=int(np.exp(rng.uniform(np.log(1), np.log(maxw)))) if maxw>1 else 1
   if width>n: width=n
   start=int(rng.integers(0, n-width+1))
   factor=float(np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, sigma)))
   factor = min(1000.0, max(1e-6, factor))
   cand[start:start+width] *= factor
   # sometimes mirror? no
   # occasionally apply all-block small multiplicative noise
   if rng.random()<0.1:
      cand *= float(np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, sigma*0.3)))
   return _normalize(cand)
```

       if i!=j and a[i] > a[j]: break
   else: return cand

   amt=frac*a[i]
   if amt<=0 or not finite or amt>a[i]: return cand
   cand[i]-=amt; cand[j]+=amt

```python
import time
import numpy as np


"""
Searches nonnegative step-function heights for a fixed length n by:
1) building explicit good seeds (one-sided binomial square-root seed, ramps, smooth random shapes);
2) applying a subgradient pressure move that shifts mass away from active convolution peaks;
3) mixing in random block/pair moves and simulated-annealing acceptance;
4) periodically checking the exact evaluate_sequence objective.
"""


def _normalize(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    if a.size == 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=np.float64)
    a = a.copy()
    a[~np.isfinite(a)] = 0.0
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if np.isfinite(s) and s > 0.0:
        a = a / s
    else:
        a = np.full(a.shape, 1.0 / max(1, a.size), dtype=np.float64)
    return a


def _softmax(y):
    y = np.asarray(y, dtype=np.float64)
    y = y.copy()
    y[~np.isfinite(y)] = -700.0
    m = float(y.max())
    z = np.exp(y - m)
    s = float(z.sum())
    if np.isfinite(s) and s > 0.0:
        return z / s
    return np.full(y.shape, 1.0 / max(1, y.size), dtype=np.float64)


def _fft_conv_square(a):
    n = int(a.size)
    need = 2 * n - 1
    N = 1
    while N < need:
        N <<= 1
    fa = np.fft.rfft(a, N)
    c = np.fft.irfft(fa * fa, N)[:need]
    c = np.asarray(c, dtype=np.float64)
    c[~np.isfinite(c)] = 0.0
    c = np.maximum(c, 0.0)
    return c


def _surrogate_score(a, n=None):
    if n is None:
        n = int(a.size)
    s = float(np.sum(a))
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-300:
        return float("inf")
    c = _fft_conv_square(a)
    m = float(c.max())
    if not np.isfinite(m) or m <= 0.0:
        return float("inf")
    return float(2.0 * n * m / (s * s))


def _pressure_fft(a, active):
    n = int(a.size)
    need = 3 * n - 2
    N = 1
    while N < need:
        N <<= 1
    ar = a[::-1]
    fa = np.fft.rfft(ar, N)
    fv = np.fft.rfft(active.astype(np.float64), N)
    conv = np.fft.irfft(fa * fv, N)
    p = conv[n - 1:2 * n - 1]
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64)
    p[~np.isfinite(p)] = 0.0
    p = np.maximum(p, 0.0)
    return p


def _pressure_update(a, y, eta, gap, rng):
    n = int(a.size)
    c = _fft_conv_square(a)
    U = float(c.max())
    if not np.isfinite(U) or U <= 0.0:
        return a, y
    thr = U * (1.0 - gap)
    active = c >= thr
    if not np.any(active):
        active[int(np.argmax(c))] = True
    p = _pressure_fft(a, active)
    pm = float(p.max())
    if not np.isfinite(pm) or pm <= 1e-300:
        return a, y
    y2 = y - eta * (p / pm)
    y2 += rng.normal(0.0, 1e-5, n)
    y2 = np.clip(y2, -50.0, 50.0)
    cand = _softmax(y2)
    return cand, y2


def _block_update(a, rng, sigma):
    n = int(a.size)
    cand = a.copy()
    maxw = max(1, n // 4)
    if maxw > 1:
        width = int(np.exp(rng.uniform(0.0, np.log(maxw))))
    else:
        width = 1
    width = max(1, min(n, width))
    start = int(rng.integers(0, n - width + 1))
    factor = float(np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, sigma)))
    factor = min(1000.0, max(1e-8, factor))
    cand[start:start + width] *= factor
    if rng.random() < 0.15:
        glob = float(np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, max(1e-6, 0.25 * sigma))))
        cand *= min(1000.0, max(1e-8, glob))
    return _normalize(cand)


def _transfer_update(a, rng):
    n = int(a.size)
    cand = a.copy()
    chosen = False
    for _ in range(30):
        i = int(rng.integers(0, n))
        j = int(rng.integers(0, n))
        if i != j and a[i] > a[j]:
            chosen = True
            break
    if not chosen:
        return cand
    frac = float(rng.uniform(0.01, 0.25))
    amt = frac * a[i]
    if not np.isfinite(amt) or amt <= 0.0 or amt > a[i]:
        return cand
    cand[i] -= amt
    cand[j] += amt
    return _normalize(cand)


def _binomial_prefix(n):
    a = np.empty(n, dtype=np.float64)
    a[0] = 1.0
    for k in range(1, n):
        a[k] = a[k - 1] * (2.0 * k - 1.0) / (2.0 * k)
    return a


def _insert_top(top, score, arr, limit):
    if not np.isfinite(score):
        return
    i = 0
    while i < len(top):
        if score < top[i][0]:
            break
        i += 1
    top.insert(i, (score, arr))
    if len(top) > limit:
        top.pop()


def _consider_candidate(top, arr, limit):
    try:
        a = _normalize(arr)
        if a.size == 0:
            return
        s = _surrogate_score(a)
        if np.isfinite(s):
            _insert_top(top, s, a, limit)
    except Exception:
        return


def _random_poly_candidates(top, base, x, xp, rng, count, time_limit, limit):
    n = int(base.size)
    for _ in range(count):
        if time.monotonic() > time_limit:
            break
        scale = float(rng.choice([0.2, 0.5, 1.0, 2.0, 4.0]))
        theta = rng.normal(0.0, scale, len(xp))
        v = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
        for i in range(len(xp)):
            v += float(theta[i]) * xp[i]
        v = np.clip(v, -20.0, 20.0)
        cand = base * np.exp(v)
        _consider_candidate(top, cand, limit)


def _random_osci_candidates(top, base, x, rng, count, time_limit, limit):
    n = int(base.size)
    for _ in range(count):
        if time.monotonic() > time_limit:
            break
        v = np.full(n, float(rng.normal(0.0, 0.5)), dtype=np.float64)
        terms = int(rng.integers(1, 5))
        for _t in range(terms):
            f = float(rng.uniform(1.0, 30.0))
            ph = float(rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi))
            amp = float(rng.normal(0.0, float(rng.choice([0.3, 0.8, 1.5, 3.0]))))
            v += amp * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * f * x + ph)
            if rng.random() < 0.5:
                v += 0.5 * amp * np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * f * x + ph)
        v = np.clip(v, -20.0, 20.0)
        cand = base * np.exp(v)
        _consider_candidate(top, cand, limit)


def _local_search(a0, rng, deadline, start_exact_score):
    a = _normalize(a0)
    n = int(a.size)
    best = a.copy()
    best_score = _surrogate_score(best)
    best_exact = float(start_exact_score) if np.isfinite(start_exact_score) else float("inf")
    best_exact_a = best.copy()

    y = np.log(np.maximum(a, 1e-300))
    anneal_t = 0.05
    no_improve = 0
    last_exact = time.monotonic()

    etas = np.array([0.001, 0.003, 0.008, 0.02, 0.05, 0.12], dtype=np.float64)
    gaps = np.array([0.02, 0.05, 0.10, 0.18, 0.30, 0.50], dtype=np.float64)
    sigmas = np.array([0.10, 0.25, 0.50, 1.00, 2.00], dtype=np.float64)

    it = 0
    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        it += 1
        if it % 2500 == 0:
            anneal_t *= 0.85
            if anneal_t < 1e-5:
                anneal_t = 1e-5

        move = float(rng.random())
        if move < 0.62:
            eta = float(etas[int(rng.integers(len(etas)))])
            gap = float(gaps[int(rng.integers(len(gaps)))])
            cand, y_new = _pressure_update(a, y, eta, gap, rng)
        elif move < 0.86:
            sigma = float(sigmas[int(rng.integers(len(sigmas)))])
            cand = _block_update(a, rng, sigma)
            y_new = np.log(np.maximum(cand, 1e-300))
        else:
            cand = _transfer_update(a, rng)
            y_new = np.log(np.maximum(cand, 1e-300))

        if cand is None:
            continue

        cand = _normalize(cand)
        score = _surrogate_score(cand)
        if not np.isfinite(score):
            continue

        delta = score - best_score
        accept = False
        if delta < -1e-12:
            accept = True
        else:
            if anneal_t > 0.0 and delta < 50.0 * anneal_t:
                x = delta / anneal_t
                prob = float(np.exp(-x)) if x < 700.0 else 0.0
                if rng.random() < prob:
                    accept = True

        if accept:
            a = cand
            y = y_new
            if delta < -1e-12:
                best = a.copy()
                best_score = score
                no_improve = 0
                now = time.monotonic()
                if (score < best_exact - 0.001) or (now - last_exact > 30.0):
                    ex = evaluate_sequence(best.tolist())
                    if np.isfinite(ex) and ex < best_exact:
                        best_exact = float(ex)
                        best_exact_a = best.copy()
                    last_exact = now
            else:
                no_improve += 1
        else:
            no_improve += 1

        if no_improve > 4000:
            a = _block_update(best, rng, 2.0)
            y = np.log(np.maximum(a, 1e-300))
            no_improve = 0

    final_candidates = [best, best_exact_a, a]
    final_best_a = best_exact_a
    final_best_score = best_exact

    for arr in final_candidates:
        try:
            arr = _normalize(arr)
            ex = evaluate_sequence(arr.tolist())
            if np.isfinite(ex) and ex < final_best_score:
                final_best_score = float(ex)
                final_best_a = arr
        except Exception:
            continue

    return final_best_a, final_best_score


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=1800, **kwargs):
    start = time.monotonic()
    total_budget = float(max(60.0, min(1800.0, float(budget_s))))
    hard_end = start + total_budget - 5.0

    rng = np.random.default_rng(int(seed))

    try:
        parent_seq = height_sequence_1
        n = int(len(parent_seq))
    except Exception:
        parent_seq = None
        n = 6733

    if n <= 0:
        n = 6733

    if parent_seq is None:
        parent = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
    else:
        parent = _normalize(parent_seq)
        if parent.size != n:
            tmp = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
            m = min(parent.size, n)
            tmp[:m] = parent[:m]
            parent = _normalize(tmp)

    x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n, dtype=np.float64)
    xp = [np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)]
    for _ in range(5):
        xp.append(xp[-1] * x)

    p = _binomial_prefix(n)
    ramp = np.arange(1, n + 1, dtype=np.float64)

    top = []
    lim = 8

    _consider_candidate(top, parent, lim)
    _consider_candidate(top, p, lim)
    _consider_candidate(top, p[::-1], lim)
    _consider_candidate(top, p + p[::-1], lim)
    _consider_candidate(top, np.ones(n), lim)
    _consider_candidate(top, ramp, lim)
    _consider_candidate(top, ramp[::-1], lim)
    _consider_candidate(top, ramp + ramp[::-1], lim)

    powers = [0.25, 0.5, 0.75, 1.0, 1.25, 1.5, 2.0]
    for pw in powers:
        _consider_candidate(top, p ** pw, lim)

    lams = [-3.0, -1.5, -0.5, -0.2, 0.0, 0.2, 0.5, 1.0, 2.0, 4.0]
    for lam in lams:
        _consider_candidate(top, p * np.exp(lam * x), lim)
        _consider_candidate(top, np.exp(lam * x), lim)

    param_time = max(30.0, min(240.0, 0.13 * total_budget))
    param_deadline = start + param_time
    if param_deadline > hard_end:
        param_deadline = hard_end - 15.0

    _random_poly_candidates(top, p, x, xp, rng, 2200, param_deadline, lim)
    _random_poly_candidates(top, np.ones(n), x, xp, rng, 1200, param_deadline, lim)
    _random_poly_candidates(top, ramp, x, xp, rng, 700, param_deadline, lim)
    _random_osci_candidates(top, p, x, rng, 700, param_deadline, lim)
    _random_osci_candidates(top, np.ones(n), x, rng, 500, param_deadline, lim)

    exact_top = []
    for _, arr in top[:5]:
        try:
            ex = evaluate_sequence(arr.tolist())
            _insert_top(exact_top, float(ex), arr, lim)
        except Exception:
            continue

    if len(exact_top) == 0 and top:
        global_seq = top[0][1]
        try:
            global_score = float(evaluate_sequence(global_seq.tolist()))
        except Exception:
            global_score = float(top[0][0])
        return [float(v) for v in _normalize(global_seq)]

    global_seq = exact_top[0][1]
    global_score = float(exact_top[0][0])
    print("initial best exact score: %.10f" % global_score, flush=True)

    seeds = exact_top[:3]
    if len(seeds) == 1:
        fracs = [1.0]
    elif len(seeds) == 2:
        fracs = [0.70, 0.30]
    else:
        fracs = [0.65, 0.25, 0.10]

    remaining = max(5.0, hard_end - time.monotonic())
    for idx, (_, arr) in enumerate(seeds):
        if time.monotonic() >= hard_end - 10.0:
            break
        frac = fracs[idx]
        alloc = frac * remaining
        local_deadline = min(hard_end, time.monotonic() + alloc)
        if local_deadline <= time.monotonic() + 2.0:
            continue
        try:
            best_arr, best_ex = _local_search(arr, rng, local_deadline, float(evaluate_sequence(arr.tolist())))
        except Exception:
            continue
        if np.isfinite(best_ex) and best_ex < global_score:
            global_score = float(best_ex)
            global_seq = best_arr
            print("local search improved exact score to: %.10f" % global_score, flush=True)

    final = _normalize(global_seq)
    if final.size != n:
        tmp = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
        m = min(final.size, n)
        tmp[:m] = final[:m]
        final = _normalize(tmp)

    try:
        final_score = float(evaluate_sequence(final.tolist()))
        if not np.isfinite(final_score):
            final = _normalize(parent)
            if final.size != n:
                tmp = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
                m = min(final.size, n)
                tmp[:m] = final[:m]
                final = _normalize(tmp)
        else:
            print("final exact score: %.10f" % final_score, flush=True)
    except Exception:
        final = _normalize(parent)
        if final.size != n:
            tmp = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
            m = min(final.size, n)
            tmp[:m] = final[:m]
            final = _normalize(tmp)

    return [float(v) for v in final]
```
